In Osaka, eighth month of 1783, three Japanese adults share this second-floor merchant chamber: Hana, twenty-nine, with narrow mature jaw, hair piled high with two ivory pins, her beni-painted lower lip parted in surprised laughter, eyes crinkled toward the mask, her nadeshiko-patterned kimono kosoed across lap while the left shoulder and breast remain bare above displaced under-robe; Kiyonori, thirty-two, broad forehead, close-cropped merchant's sides with topknot, mouth open in delighted shout, gaze flying between wife and companion, his indigo outer robe slid from shoulders to bunch at elbows; Toshimasa, forty, rounded mature cheeks, gray-threaded topknot, half-lidded warm eyes, slight smile, ai-indigo haori with persimmon lining visible at collar, his spread hand on Kiyonori's waist an open declaration. The fox mask with gold mineral flecks and red lacquer grin occupies the lower prop strip beside a spilled Go stone box and scattered black and white stones; the central band compresses the three bodies in a triangular embrace of gazes; the upper strip shows a half-open shoji with a tanuki-shaped iron kettle on the lintel beyond, its eye seeming to glint in the joke. Hair masses and garment edges provide keyblock-like contour separation: Hana's dark piled hair a rounded silhouette against the upper shoji, Kiyonori's displaced jinbaori sleeve a sharp diagonal cutting across center, Toshimasa's haori hem a horizontal anchor; crisp overlap places Toshimasa's gesturing hand before Kiyonori's shoulder, Hana's mask-lift before her own breast, the writing desk rail before Kiyonori's hips; near-uniform scale keeps all three heads in the upper half of center; a dominant diagonal runs from Hana's raised mask hand through Kiyonori's reaching arm to Toshimasa's planted feet. The palette holds ai indigo in Toshimasa's haori and Kiyonori's under-robe, beni red in Hana's lips and mask interior, persimmon in the obi cord and haori lining, unbleached washi ground in the tatami and shoji paper, malachite mineral accent flecked on the mask; all are flat contour-enclosed matte fields with sumi-black piping at every garment edge; skin is one warm base with one bounded beni blush on Hana's exposed breast and cheek; one bokashi gradient shifts from pale washi through smoke-touched gray to indigo darkness across the upper shoji strip only, suggesting distant evening. Close-read cues: Toshimasa's individual mature face shape with heavier eyelid fold than Kiyonori's, and Hana's nadeshiko-patterned sleeve seam registered as repeating pale five-petaled shapes against indigo ground. Physical set craft includes visible washi fiber in the blank paper panel above the desk and burnished lacquer on the Go stone box lid. Material patina: beni on Hana's obi cord faded at the outer curve where fingers have untied it repeatedly, the frequently touched lacquer edge of the Go box worn pale at one corner, smoke-darkened upper shoji frame from years of andon lamp use, compressed tatami mat at Hana's left knee with flattened weave pattern.
